Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the affine framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 269. The resulting operator achieves bound O(118/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by affine generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 269, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 118. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the affine structure using the extension principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 193. Each subproblem has a solution by the extension principle fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
